## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试，或者帮你查一查邮件和日程里需要留意的事。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos while you do something else.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 바로 할 수 있는 일로는, 예를 들어 어떤 저장소에서 깨진 테스트를 찾아 고치는 일을 맡길 수 있어요.

오늘은 메일이나 일정 확인, 자료 조사, 코드 작업처럼 필요한 걸 말씀해 주시면 돼요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```

  [partner message] 안녕히 주무세요. 내일 아침에 봐요.

```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは信頼性のある通信(届いたか確認し、順番どおりに届ける)を、UDPは速さ優先で確認なしに送る通信です。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar o actualizar tu propia rama antes de compartirla, porque deja un historial lineal y limpio. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es pública o la comparten otros, porque reescribir commits compartidos rompe su trabajo.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、有序的字节流：收发前要先握手，丢了会重传，保证数据完整送到；UDP 则只管尽快发出去，不保证送达和顺序，但没有握手和重传的开销，所以更快、更轻。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own local branch before sharing it, to keep history linear and clean. Merge when the branch is already shared or you want the record of when work was integrated, since rebasing published history forces others to fix their copies.
```

```

```


### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to the database on disk before the change is applied to the data itself. If the system crashes midway, it replays that log on restart so committed work survives and half-finished work is rolled back.
```

```
完成标准：
- 按 review 意见改完锁粒度，代码改动只限于这一处相关逻辑；
- 跑一遍相关测试，把测试输出作为证据报告；
- 说明改了哪些锁、缩小到了哪里。

不做的事：不合并，不推送到远端，不改 PR 的状态。改动提交在这个任务自己的分支上即可，推送要等用户点头。

地点：沿用这个任务原来的仓库和分支。
```

```
  [an app card under the message: made "京沪高铁周五票价", with Open] the app's file now (v2, 1481 characters): <main class="k-app">
<section class="k-sum">
<div class="k-sum-label">周五北京到上海 · 600 元以下</div>
<div class="k-sum-value" id="sum">—</div>
<div class="k-sum-sub" id="sumsub">还没查</div>
</section>
<h2 class="k-h">今天的班次</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<p class="k-from" id="from"></p>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary><p class="k-from">每天的票价会自动更新在这里。</p></details>
</main>
<script>
function esc(s){return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]));}
function draw(d){
  d=d||{};
  var t=d.trains||[];
  var under=t.filter(x=>x.price<600);
  document.getElementById('sum').textContent=under.length?under.length+' 班':'没有';
  document.getElementById('sumsub').textContent=under.length?under.map(x=>x.no+' '+x.price+' 元').join('，'):'周五暂时没有 600 元以下的班次';
  document.getElementById('list').innerHTML=t.map(x=>{
    var muted=x.price>=600?' style="color:var(--d-muted)"':'';
    return '<div class="k-row"><div class="k-row-main"'+muted+'>'+esc(x.no)+'<div class="k-row-sub">'+esc(x.seat||'')+'</div></div><div class="k-row-value"'+muted+'>'+x.price+' 元</div></div>';
  }).join('');
  document.getElementById('from').textContent=d.from||'';
}
ducter.onChange(draw);
ducter.get('trains').then?ducter.get('trains').then(function(){draw({trains:ducter.get('trains'),from:ducter.get('from')});}):draw({trains:ducter.get('trains'),from:ducter.get('from')});
```

```
}
```

```
  <ul class="k-list" id="list"></ul>
</div>

<script>
function fmt(n){return "¥"+n.toLocaleString("zh-CN");}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  const total=document.getElementById("total");
  const sub=document.getElementById("sub");
  list.innerHTML="";
  let sum=0;
  for(const x of xs){
    sum+=Number(x.yuan)||0;
    const li=document.createElement("li");
    li.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("span");
    main.className="k-row-main";
    main.textContent=x.what;
    const sb=document.createElement("span");
    sb.className="k-row-sub";
    sb.textContent=x.day;
    const val=document.createElement("span");
    val.className="k-row-value";
    val.textContent=fmt(Number(x.yuan)||0);
    li.appendChild(main);li.appendChild(sb);li.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(li);
  }
  if(xs.length){
    total.textContent=fmt(sum);
    sub.textContent="共 "+xs.length+" 笔";
  }else{
    total.className="k-sum-value k-none";
    total.textContent="还没记";
    sub.textContent="记了花销就会合计在这里";
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length? xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(sum) : "还没记");
}
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}
```

```
      <in … r('click', e => {
  const row = e.target.closest('[data-id]'); if (!row) return;
  const x = items.find(i => i.id === row.dataset.id); if (!x) return;
  editId = x.id; cat = x.cat;
  $('#amount').value = x.amount; $('#note').value = x.note || '';
  $('#formTitle').textContent = 'Change expense';
  $('#save').textContent = 'Save';
  $('#del').hidden = false; $('#cancel').hidden = false;
  $('#addbox').open = true;
  renderChips();
  $('#amount').focus();
});

$('#cancel').addEventListener('click', resetForm);

$('#del').addEventListener('click', () => {
  items = items.filter(i => i.id !== editId);
  save(); resetForm();
});

$('#form').addEventListener('submit', e => {
  e.preventDefault();
  const amount = parseFloat($('#amount').value);
  if (!(amount > 0)) { $('#amount').focus(); return; }
  const note = $('#note').value.trim();
  if (editId) {
    const x = items.find(i => i.id === editId);
    if (x) { x.amount = amount; x.note = note; x.cat = cat; }
  } else {
    items.push({ id: Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6), amount, note, cat, date: today() });
  }
  save(); resetForm();
});

renderChips();
load();
ducter.onChange(load);
</script>
```

```
</style>
<main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">今天喝了</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"><span class="k-none">还没记</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">记下每一次，这里会合计</div>
  </section>
  <h2 class="k-h">今天的每一次</h2>
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty">还没记。喝完一次，在下面记一笔。</div>
  <details class="k-add" id="addbox">
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-form">
      <div class="k-field"><label>时间（如 06:10）</label><input class="k-input" id="t" placeholder="06:10"></div>
      <div class="k-field"><label>喝了多少 ml</label><input class="k-num" id="ml" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="120"></div>
      <div class="k-btns">
        <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记下</button>
        <button class="k-btn" id="cancel" hidden>取消</button>
        <button class="k-btn" id="del" hidden>删掉</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </details>
</main>
<script>
(function(){
  var KEY='feeds';
  var feeds=[];
  var edit … $('del').hidden=true;
    $('cancel').hidden=true;
  }
  function edit(f){
    editing=f;
    $('t').value=f.time;
    $('ml').value=f.ml;
    $('save').textContent='改好了';
    $('del').hidden=false;
    $('cancel').hidden=false;
    $('addbox').open=true;
  }
  $('save').addEventListener('click',function(){
    var m=$('t').value.trim().match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/);
    var ml=parseFloat($('ml').value);
    if(!m || !(ml>0)) return;
    var time=String(m[1]).padStart(2,'0')+':'+m[2];
    if(editing){
      editing.time=time;
      editing.ml=ml;
    }else{
      feeds.push({date:today(), time:time, ml:ml});
    }
    persist();
    reset();
    $('addbox').open=false;
    render();
  });
  $('cancel').addEventListener('click',function(){
    reset();
    $('addbox').open=false;
  });
  $('del').addEventListener('click',function(){
    if(!editing) return;
    feeds=feeds.filter(function(f){return f!==editing;});
    persist();
    reset();
    $('addbox').open=false;
    render();
  });
  function load(){
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get(KEY)).then(function(v){
      feeds=Array.isArray(v)?v:[];
      render();
    });
  }
  ducter.onChange(load);
  reset();
  load();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"date":"2026-11-23","time":"06:10","ml":120},{"date":"2026-11-23","time":"09:05","ml":90}]}
```

```
  <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">4</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub">Tick each one off when it lands</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">My PRs</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>Everything has landed.</div>
</main>
<style>
  .k-row{display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:flex-start;cursor:pointer}
  .k-row-main{flex:1;min-width:0}
  .k-row.done .k-row-main{opacity:.5;text-decoration:line-through}
  .k-box{width:22px;height:22px;flex:0 0 22px;border:2px solid var(--d-line,#999);border-radius:6px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;margin-top:2px;color:var(--d-good,#2a7);font-weight:700}
  .k-row.done .k-box{border-color:var(--d-good,#2a7);background:var(--d-sunken,#eee)}
  .k-tag{color:var(--d-muted,#777);font-size:13px}
  .k-title{color:var(--d-ink,#111);font-weight:600}
</style>
<script>
  var seed = ducter.get("seed") || [];
  var items = seed;
  function esc(s){return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return {"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;"}[c]})}
  function render(){
    var list = document.getElementById("list");
    list.innerHTML = items.map(function(p,i){
      return '<div class="k-row'+(p.done?' done':'')+'" data-i="'+i+'">'
        + '<div class="k-box">'+(p.done?'✓':'')+'</div>'
        + '<div class="k-row-main"><div class="k-title">'+esc(p.title)+'</div>'
        + '<div class="k-tag">'+esc(p.repo)+'</div>'
        + '<div class="k-row-sub">'+esc(p.waiting)+'</div></div></div>';
    }).join("");
    var open = items.filter(function(p){return !p.done}).length;
    document.getElementById("count").textContent = open;
    document.getElementById("empty").hidden = open > 0;
    ducter.summary(open + " waiting · " + (items.length-open) + " landed");
  }
  function save(){ ducter.set("prs", items); }
  document.getElementById("list").addEventListener("click", function(e){
    var row = e.target.closest(".k-row"); if(!row) return;
    var p = items[+row.getAttribute("data-i")];
    p.done = !p.done; save(); render();
  });
  var got = ducter.get("prs");
  Promise.resolve(got).then(function(v){ if(Array.isArray(v) && v.length){ items = v; } render(); });
  ducter.onChange(function(){ Promise.resolve(ducter.get("prs")).then(function(v){ if(Array.isArray(v)){ items = v; render(); } }); });
</script>
```

### reply-older-task-zh

```
(earlier) user: 修一下 ~/dev/foo 里 TTL 那个时好时坏的测试
(earlier) partner ack: 我去看看 foo 里那个时好时坏的 TTL 测试，弄清楚了告诉你。  → task "foo: TTL 测试不稳定" done; its report: The TTL test waited on real time for a 1s expiry; under load it fired early. Switched it to a fake clock; 20 runs in a row pass.
(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我让改 TTL 测试的那个任务再补一条正好到期那一刻的测试，弄清楚了告诉你。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话：「这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻」

在这次修复的基础上（假时钟），再加一条测试，覆盖 TTL 正好到期的那一刻：
- 时钟推进到恰好等于过期时间的时刻，该值应当已经过期（或按现有约定判定，并在测试里写清楚边界是包含还是不包含）。
- 时钟比过期时间少一毫秒（或最小单位）时，值仍然有效。
- 沿用假时钟，不使用真实 sleep 或计时。

完成标准：新测试通过，原有 TTL 测试仍然通过；把测试输出和边界约定的结论写进报告。
不要合并，不要推送，不要开 PR。在原来的分支上提交即可。
地点：~/dev/foo 的原仓库，分支 ducter/foo-ttl。
```
